

send_child_to_holy_order_tooltip_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = order_member
		add_piety_level = 1
		custom_tooltip = character_will_leave_court_holy_order
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				scope:recipient = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

send_child_to_holy_order_effect = {
	$GRANDMASTER$ = {
		add_courtier = scope:recipient
		if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:actor
                    NOT = {
                    	has_trait = bastard
                    }
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 25
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:actor
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 15
            }
        }
	}
	scope:recipient = {
		add_trait = order_member
		add_piety_level = 1
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			depose = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				faith != $GRANDMASTER$.faith
			}
			set_character_faith = $GRANDMASTER$.faith
			set_character_rite = $GRANDMASTER$.rite
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = scope:recipient
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				scope:recipient = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
	take_vows_faith_opinion_effect = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			$GRANDMASTER$ = {
				has_realm_law = cultural_holy_order_succession_law
				NOT = {
					culture = {
						has_same_culture_heritage = scope:recipient.culture
					}
				}
			}
		}
		$GRANDMASTER$ = { save_scope_as = grandmaster }
		custom_tooltip = military_order_wrong_culture_tt
	}
}

send_child_to_clergy_effect = {
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = pam_simony_paid_vows }
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						scope:demand ?= yes
						AND = {
							is_imprisoned_by = scope:puppet_or_actor
							OR = {
								is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
								any_claim = {
									exists = this
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = forced_taking_vows
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
			else = {
				add_opinion = {
					modifier = demanded_taking_vows
					target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
			}
		}
		simplified_clergy_effect = { RECIPIENT = scope:recipient }
	}
	scope:puppet_or_actor = {
		#Spiritual fulfillment gain
   		if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            NOT = {
    	        		has_trait = bastard
    	        	}
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else_if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            trigger_if = {
    	            	limit = {
    	            		has_trait = bastard
    	            	}
    	            	is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            }
    	            OR = {
    	            	house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
    	            	is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            }
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else_if = {
   			limit = {
   				scope:recipient = {
    	            trigger_if = {
    	            	limit = {
    	            		has_trait = bastard
    	            	}
    	            	OR = {
    	            		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
    	            		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	           		}
    	            }
    	            trigger_else = {
    	            	OR = {
    	            		dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
    	            		is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
    	            	}
    	            }
    	        }
   			}
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
   		else = {
   			change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   				value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   				multiply = 0.5
   				if = {
   					limit = {
   						scope:recipient = {
   							is_imprisoned = yes
   						}
   					}
   					multiply = 0.5
   				}
   			}
   		}
	}
}



simplified_clergy_effect = {
	$RECIPIENT$ = {
		# Full monastic vows supersede a deacon's ordination
		remove_trait = cleric
		add_trait = devoted
		add_piety_level = 1
		if = {
			limit = { is_ruler = yes }
			depose = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_married = yes
			}
			every_spouse = {
				divorce = $RECIPIENT$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = betrothed
			}
			break_betrothal = betrothed
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
				}
				break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				is_concubine = yes
			}
			this.concubinist = {
				remove_concubine = $RECIPIENT$
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				number_of_concubines > 0
			}
			every_concubine = {
				$RECIPIENT$ = {
					remove_concubine = prev
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

release_as_executioner_effect = {
	scope:actor = {
		court_position_grant_effect = {
			EMPLOYER = scope:actor
			POS = executioner
			CANDIDATE = scope:recipient
		}
		save_scope_as = liege
	}
}

create_monastic_decision_effect = {
	save_scope_as = root_scope
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message_tenet
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_realm_law = church_authority_1
				has_realm_law = church_authority_2
				has_realm_law = church_authority_3
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_gain_hook
	}
	custom_tooltip = create_monastic_order_decision_effect_message_warning

	change_spiritual_fulfillment = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
	stress_impact = {
		zealous = minor_stress_impact_loss
		impatient = miniscule_stress_impact_gain
		patient = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
		calm = miniscule_stress_impact_loss
	}

	if = { # If you have no valid barony this will throw errors when you open the decision - we add this check to avoid that 
		limit = {
			exists = scope:title
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:title = {
				ordered_county_province = {
					order_by = {
						value = 0
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									terrain = hills
									terrain = mountains
									terrain = desert_mountains
									terrain = desert
								}
							}
							add = 2
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								OR = {
									terrain = forest
									terrain = drylands
									terrain = wetlands
									terrain = jungle
									terrain = terraced_hills
								}
							}
							add = 1
						}
					}
					limit = {
						has_holding = no
						has_ongoing_construction = no
					}
					begin_create_holding = church_holding
					save_scope_as = province
					set_variable = {
						name = monastic_order_capital
						value = root.faith
						years = 50
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	trigger_event = holy_order.2009
}

create_monastic_order_leader_and_notification_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:chosen_leader
		}
		scope:chosen_leader = {
			save_scope_as = new_monastic_leader
		}
	}
	else = {
		scope:leader = { save_scope_as = new_monastic_leader }
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		if = { # set up currencies for the HO leader, has to be saved as scope:leader
			limit = {
				exists = scope:new_monastic_leader
			}
			scope:new_monastic_leader = {
				set_variable = founding_monastic
			}
			scope:new_holy_order.leader.primary_title = {
				set_variable = {
					name = founder
					value = scope:founder
				}
			}
			#If we have to transfer Order from default leader to someone else
			if = {
				limit = {
					scope:new_monastic_leader = {
						has_character_flag = created_char
					}
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = {
					remove_character_flag = created_char
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						scope:new_holy_order.leader ?= scope:new_monastic_leader 
					}
				}
				scope:new_holy_order.leader.primary_title = {
					create_title_and_vassal_change = {
						type = granted
						save_scope_as = change
						add_claim_on_loss = yes
					}
					change_title_holder_include_vassals = {
						holder = scope:new_monastic_leader
						change = scope:change
					}
					resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = {
					every_courtier = {
						add_trait = devoted
					}
				}
				
			}
			
			scope:new_monastic_leader = {
				add_gold = 100 #So that they have some money to lend out
				add_piety_level = 2
				add_gold = holy_order_starting_gold
			}
			every_in_list = {
				list = grabbed_monks
				limit = {
					NOR = {
						this = scope:new_monastic_leader
						is_courtier_of = scope:new_monastic_leader
					}
				}
				scope:new_monastic_leader = { add_courtier = prev }
				remove_character_flag = created_char
			}
			
			# send messages
			send_interface_toast = {
				type = monastic_holy_order_founded_message
				desc = i_created_monastic_holy_order_message
				custom_tooltip = monastic_order_tenet_advocated_tt
				left_icon = scope:new_monastic_leader
				right_icon = scope:new_holy_order.title
			}
		}
		# send messages to relevant characters that HO got created
		save_scope_as = holy_order_creator
		every_player = {
			limit = {
				faith = scope:holy_order_creator.faith
				this != scope:holy_order_creator
			}
			send_interface_message = {
				type = monastic_holy_order_founded_message
				desc = someone_created_monastic_holy_order_message
				custom_tooltip = monastic_order_tenet_advocated_tt
				left_icon = scope:holy_order_creator
				right_icon = scope:new_holy_order.title
			}
		}
	}
}

create_monastic_order_founder_effect = {
	# gain opinion with your HoF, if you have one and it's not you
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = faith.religious_head
			faith.religious_head != root
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = root
				modifier = founded_monastic_order_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
	}
	# get a nice modifier
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_character_modifier = founded_monastic_order_modifier }
		}
		add_character_modifier = {
			modifier = founded_monastic_order_modifier
			years = 5
		}
	}
	# boost the fervor
	faith = {
		change_fervor = {
			value = notable_fervor_gain
			desc = fervor_gain_monastic_order_founded
		}
	}

	change_spiritual_fulfillment = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value

	scope:leader = {
		save_scope_as = order_master
	}

	create_character_memory = {
		type = founded_monastic_order_memory
		participants = {
			order_master = scope:leader
		}
	}

	# PAM - Catalysts
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_pam_dlc_trigger = yes
			religion = religion:christianity_religion
			situation:the_christian_church ?= {
				situation_top_has_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_founded_monastic_order
			}
		}
		situation:the_christian_church = {
			trigger_situation_catalyst = catalyst_the_christian_church_founded_monastic_order
		}
	}
}

grab_best_monk_for_tenet_effect = {
	$TENET$ = {
		save_scope_as = monk_grabbin_tenet
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = dynasty
		}
		dynasty = {
			ordered_dynasty_member = {
				order_by = learning
				#Family member courtier
				limit = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:founder
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					OR = {
						rite = {
							rite_has_tenet = scope:monk_grabbin_tenet
						}
						has_personal_tenet = scope:monk_grabbin_tenet
					}
				}
				#Family member
				alternative_limit = {
					is_close_family_of = scope:founder
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				#House member courtier
				alternative_limit = {
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
				}
				#House member
				alternative_limit = {
					house ?= scope:founder.house
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				#dynasty member courtier
				alternative_limit = {
					is_courtier_of = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
				}
				#Dynasty member
				alternative_limit = {
					is_ruler = no
					in_diplomatic_range = scope:founder
					potential_abbot_trigger = yes
					#Don't steal players' courtiers
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							is_courtier = yes
							NOT = {
								is_courtier_of = scope:founder
							}
						}
						NOT = {
							liege = {
								is_ai = no
							}
						}
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:abbot_prospect
			}
		}
		#Grab monk courtiers ordered by family importance
		ordered_courtier = {
			order_by = learning
			#Bastards first
			limit = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:founder
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			#friends
			alternative_limit = {
				has_any_good_relationship_with_character_trigger = { CHARACTER = scope:founder }
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			alternative_limit = {
				potential_abbot_trigger = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				exists = scope:abbot_prospect
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			#male monk that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male lowborn monk that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					dynasty = none
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male lowborn monk that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					dynasty = none
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#male monk that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = female_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = male
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
			#female nun that belongs to local culture
			2 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = male_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = female
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}

			#female nun that belongs to founder culture
			1 = {
				trigger = {
					trigger_if = {
						limit = {
							exists = scope:leader.primary_title
						}
						scope:leader.primary_title = {
							NOT = { has_variable = male_order }
						}
					}
				}
				#Create newly-generated option
				create_character = {
					template = monastic_order_leader_character
					culture = scope:barony.county.culture
					location = scope:founder.location
					gender = female
					save_scope_as = abbot_prospect
				}
			}
		}
		scope:abbot_prospect = {
			add_character_flag = {
				flag = created_char
				days = 60
			}
		}
	}
	scope:abbot_prospect = {
		add_to_list = grabbed_monks
	}
}
pick_monastic_founder_option_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:monastic_order
		}
		custom_tooltip = monastic_tenet_pick_tt
	}
	else = {
		custom_tooltip = monastic_tenet_pick_unformed_order_tt
	}
	# holy_order.2009 stored the founder boost on the province until this order title existed
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:monastic_order
			scope:barony.title_province ?= { has_variable = new_order_ascetic_boost }
		}
		scope:barony.title_province = {
			var:new_order_ascetic_boost = { save_temporary_scope_as = new_order_ascetic_founder }
			remove_variable = new_order_ascetic_boost
		}
		scope:monastic_order = {
			set_variable = {
				name = ascetic_order_founder
				value = scope:new_order_ascetic_founder
				years = 50
			}
		}
	}
	#Gain popularity if taking holding from old order
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:target_order_tenet
		}
		faith = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_tenet_popularity_trigger = yes }
				if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 5
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 4
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 4
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 3
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 3
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 2
						}
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						list_size = {
							name = revoked_leases
							value >= 2
						}
					}
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 1.5
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					change_tenet_popularity = {
						target = scope:tenet_pick
						change = {
							value = medium_tenet_popularity_gain
							multiply = 1
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		
		if = {
			limit = {
				rite = {
					OR = {
						has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_dulia status = core }
						has_tenet_status = { tenet = tenet_dulia status = permitted }
					}
				}
			}
			custom_tooltip = monastic_founder_become_saint_tt
		}
		#Reward high church authority theocrat with a hook
		if = {
			limit = {
				root = {
					OR = {
						has_realm_law = church_authority_1
						has_realm_law = church_authority_2
						has_realm_law = church_authority_3
					}
				}
			}
		}
		root = {
			add_hook = {
				type = indebted_hook
				target = scope:chosen_leader
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = scope:target_lessee
		}
		scope:target_lessee = {
			set_relation_rival = {
				target = scope:chosen_leader
				reason = rival_shattered_order
			}
		}
	}
}

take_vows_demand_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			scope:recipient = {
				is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		add_tyranny = medium_tyranny_gain
	}
	
	scope:recipient = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_relation_rival = scope:puppet_or_actor
				}
				OR = {
					ai_zeal <= low_negative_ai_value
					ai_greed >= low_positive_ai_value
					ai_vengefulness >= low_positive_ai_value
				}
				NOR = {
					has_trait = forgiving
					has_trait = craven
					has_trait = zealous
					has_trait = content
					has_trait = humble
					has_trait = bastard
				}
			}
			set_relation_rival = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				reason = rival_forced_to_take_vows
			}
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					#Bastards with decently matching personality don't see this as betrayal
					NAND = {
						has_trait = bastard
						ai_vengefulness < low_positive_ai_value
						ai_greed < low_positive_ai_value
						ai_zeal > low_negative_ai_value
					}
				}
				progress_towards_rival_effect = {
					CHARACTER = scope:puppet_or_actor
					REASON = rival_forced_to_take_vows
					OPINION = 0
				}
			}
		}
		every_parent = {
			limit = {
				NOR = {
					this = scope:puppet_or_actor
					#Doesn't have other kid in root's line succession
					any_child = {
						NOT = {
							this = scope:recipient
						}
						is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
					}
					has_trait = zealous
				}
			}
			add_opinion = {
				modifier = forced_child_take_vows
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
	}
}

send_child_to_monastic_order_effect = {
   	# Legitimacy loss if you're doing this to your children
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			scope:recipient = { is_primary_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
			}
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = miniscule_legitimacy_gain
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = minor_legitimacy_loss
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			scope:recipient = { is_heir_of = scope:puppet_or_actor }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
			}
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = minor_legitimacy_gain
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_legitimacy = {
				value = miniscule_legitimacy_loss
				multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_legitimacy = yes
			hyperdulia_take_the_vows_trigger = yes
		}
		add_legitimacy = {
			value = medium_legitimacy_gain
			multiply = scope:puppet_or_actor.primary_title.tier
		}
	}

   	#Spiritual fulfillment gain
   	if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                NOT = {
            		has_trait = bastard
            	}
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = major_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else_if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                trigger_if = {
                	limit = {
                		has_trait = bastard
                	}
                	is_child_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
                OR = {
                	house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                	is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = medium_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else_if = {
   		limit = {
   			scope:recipient = {
                trigger_if = {
                	limit = {
                		has_trait = bastard
                	}
                	OR = {
                		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
               		}
                }
                trigger_else = {
                	OR = {
                		dynasty ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.dynasty
                		is_close_or_extended_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                	}
                }
            }
   		}
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = minor_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   	else = {
   		change_spiritual_fulfillment = {
   			value = miniscule_spiritual_fulfillment_value
   			if = {
   				limit = {
   					scope:recipient = {
   						is_imprisoned = yes
   					}
   				}
   				multiply = 0.5
   			}
   		}
   	}
   

    #Send to holy order if we can, if not... make just rando monk in a bishop's court
    scope:archabbot = {
        add_courtier = scope:recipient
        if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                    NOT = {
                    	has_trait = bastard
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
                }
            }
            add_opinion = {
                target = scope:puppet_or_actor
                modifier = pleased_opinion
                opinion = 15
            }
        }
        if = {
        	limit = {
        		scope:recipient = {
        			OR = {
                		house ?= scope:puppet_or_actor.house
                		is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
               		}
        		}
        	}
        	dynastic_monks_gain_xp_effect = yes
        }
    }

    scope:recipient = {
        add_trait = devoted
        add_piety_level = 1
        if = {
            limit = { is_ruler = yes }
            depose = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_married = yes
            }
            every_spouse = {
                divorce = scope:recipient
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                exists = betrothed
            }
            break_betrothal = betrothed
            if = {
                limit = {
                    has_been_promised_grand_wedding = yes
                }
                break_grand_wedding_betrothal_effect = yes
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_concubine = yes
            }
            this.concubinist = {
                remove_concubine = scope:recipient
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = {
                number_of_concubines > 0
            }
            every_concubine = {
                scope:recipient = {
                    remove_concubine = prev
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            house = {
                has_house_aspiration_parameter = humility_take_the_vows_bonus
            }
        }
        add_piety_experience = 100
        add_character_modifier = {
            modifier = tgp_humility_take_the_vows_bonus_modifier
            years = 10
        }
    }
}
take_vows_faith_opinion_effect = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				NOT = {
              		has_trait = bastard
              	}
			}
		}
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 20
			}
		}
	}
	#bastards
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			exists = cp:councillor_court_chaplain
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
			}
		}
		cp:councillor_court_chaplain = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			exists = faith.religious_head
			faith.religious_head != scope:puppet_or_actor
			scope:recipient = {
				is_close_family_of = scope:puppet_or_actor
				NOT = {
              		has_trait = bastard
              	}
			}
		}
		faith.religious_head = {
			add_opinion = {
				target = scope:puppet_or_actor
				modifier = pious_opinion
				opinion = 10
			}
		}
	}
}
monastic_lifestyle_xp_yearly_gain_effect = {
	 
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_5
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_4
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.9
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_3
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.8
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_2
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.7
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_learning_1
		}
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.6
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_learning_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.3
		}
	}
	#Stewardship
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_5
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.8
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_4
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.7
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_3
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.6
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_2
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.5
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_trait = education_stewardship_1
		}
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.4
		}
	}
	else = {
		add_stewardship_lifestyle_xp = {
			value = medium_lifestyle_xp
			multiply = 0.2
		}
	}
}

military_order_yearly_trait_gain_effect = {
	#Lazarites might gain a little leprosy...
	if = {
		limit = {
			primary_title = title:d_knights_of_st_lazarus
		}
		random = {
			chance = 10
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = leper }
				}
				add_to_list = lazarists
			}
			every_courtier = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { has_trait = leper }
				}
			}
			random_in_list = {
				list = lazarists
				add_trait = leper
			}
		}
	}
	#Hospital orders gain physician trait XP
	if = {
		limit = {
			primary_title = {
				OR = {
					this = title:d_knights_of_st_lazarus
					this = title:d_knights_hospitaller
					this = title:d_knights_of_the_tau
				}
			}
		}
		every_courtier = {
			limit = {
				has_trait = lifestyle_physician
			}
			add_trait_xp = {
				trait = lifestyle_physician
				value = { 1 10 }
			}
		}
		random = {
			chance = 50
			ordered_courtier = {
				order_by = learning
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_trait = lifestyle_physician
					}
				}
				add_trait = lifestyle_physician
			}
		}
	}
}
set_up_historical_monastic_orders_effect = {


############################################################################################### Benedictines


	#BENEDICTINES
	if = {
		limit = {
			#Benedictines in Italy are eternal
			always = yes
		}
		title:b_cassino = { # Montecassino
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
			character:MB_montecassino_raimondo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Raymond de Gramat, bishop of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
			character:MB_montecassino_galardo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Galard, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1286.3.15 }
			character:MB_montecassino_tommaso = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Thomas I, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1258.2.15 }
			character:MB_montecassino_riccardo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Richard, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
			character:MB_montecassino_stefano = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Stephen II, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1211.6.16 }
			character:MB_montecassino_adenulf = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Adenulf of Caserta, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1204.5.16 }
			character:MB_montecassino_adenulf = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Adenulf of Caserta, monk of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
			character:MB_montecassino_adenulf = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Adenulf of Caserta, monk of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }
			character:MB_montecassino_adenulf = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Adenulf of Caserta, monk of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1178.10.1 }
			character:benedictine_2 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Peter II, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1144.10.1 }
			character:MB_montecassino_pietro_diacono = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Peter the Deacon, librarian of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1081.4.1 }
			character:MB_montecassino_leone = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Leo of Ostia, monk and librarian of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1066.9.15 }
			character:MB_montecassino_leone = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Leo of Ostia, monk and librarian of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1018.8.1 }
			character:MB_montecassino_atenulf = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Atenulf, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 936.8.7 }
			character:MB_montecassino_adelpert = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Adelpert, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 867.1.1 }
			character:benedictine_1 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Bertharius, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		else = {
			character:MB_montecassino_tomichis = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Tomichis, abbot of Montecassino
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 867.1.1
					game_start_date < 936.8.7
				}
				add_courtier = character:pastoral_benedictine_1 # Eadric

############################################################################################### Columban Benedictines


	##Columban Benedictines
	#Houses of St Columbanus and his disciples, under the mixed Columbanian and Benedictine rule until the Aachen reforms of 816-817
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date < 936.8.7
		}
		title:b_sankt_gallen = { # Sankt Gallen
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 867.1.1 }
			character:columban_benedictine_1 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Grimald, abbot of St Gall
		}
		else = {
			character:MB_sankt_gallen_johannes = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Johannes, abbot of St Gall and Reichenau, bishop of Constance
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 867.1.1 }
				add_courtier = character:corbine_benedictine_1 # Trasulphe
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = columban_benedictine
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:columban_benedictine }
			if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 867.1.1 }
				title:b_lure = { title:c_vesoul.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Luxeuil and Lure
				title:b_corbie = { title:c_amiens.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Corbie
				title:b_kempten = { title:c_ravensburg.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Kempten
				title:b_bobbio = { title:c_tortona.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Bobbio
				title:b_dassel = { title:c_hildesheim.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Dassel
			}
			else = { # 769.1.1
				title:b_lure = { title:c_vesoul.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Luxeuil and Lure
				title:b_corbie = { title:c_amiens.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Corbie
				title:b_kempten = { title:c_ravensburg.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:columban_benedictine } # Kempten
			}
		}
	}


############################################################################################### Eremetic Benedictines


	##Eremetic Benedictines
	#the mixed pattern in Spain of older eremeticism and Benedictine
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date < 1066.9.15
		}
		title:b_lerma = { # Lerma
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = 867_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1018.8.1 }
			character:MB_ona_inigo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Iñigo, hermit and monk of San Juan de la Peña, later abbot of Oña
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 936.8.7 }
			character:73881 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Rosendo, founder of Celanova
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 867.1.1 }
			character:eremetic_benedictine_1 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Esteban
		}
		else = {
			character:MB_liebana_beato = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Beatus, monk of Liébana
		}

			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony

				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic

		}

			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted

					value = { 20 80 }

			}

				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {

					}

			}

				limit = { game_start_date >= 1018.8.1 }

				title:b_uncastillo = { title:c_alto_aragon.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Uncastillo
				title:b_villablino = { title:c_benavente_1.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Villablino
				title:b_sedano = { title:c_palencia.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Sedano
				title:b_arnedo = { title:c_logrono.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Arnedo
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 936.8.7 }
				title:b_melide = { title:c_lugo.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Melide
				title:b_leyre = { title:c_navarra.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Leyre
				title:b_riano = { title:c_asturias_de_oviedo.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Riaño
				title:b_uncastillo = { title:c_alto_aragon.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Uncastillo
				title:b_villablino = { title:c_benavente_1.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Villablino
				title:b_sedano = { title:c_palencia.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:eremetic_benedictine } # Sedano

		}

############################################################################################### Camaldolese


	#Camaldolese
	#Hermit congregation founded by St Romuald at Camaldoli in the Casentino
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 1066.9.15
		}
		title:b_camaldoli = { # Camaldoli
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_bonaventura = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Bonaventura da Fano, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_david = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # David of Settimo, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1286.3.15 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_gerardo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Gerardo, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1258.2.15 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_martino_iii = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Martino III, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_guido = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Guido II, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1211.6.16 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_guido = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Guido II, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1204.5.16 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_guido = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Guido II, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_martino_ii = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Martino II, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_placido = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Placido, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1178.10.1 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_ildebrando = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Ildebrando, prior general of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1144.10.1 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_azzo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Azzo, prior of Camaldoli
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1081.4.1 }
			character:MB_camaldoli_rodolfo = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Rodolfo, prior of Camaldoli
		}
		else = {
			character:MB_camaldoli_rustico = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Rustico, prior of Camaldoli
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1300.9.10
					game_start_date < 1337.5.24
				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_camaldoli_gerardo_forli # Gerardo da Forlì, abbot of San Michele in Borgo
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1178.10.1
					game_start_date < 1187.7.3
				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_camaldoli_placido # Placido, prior of the hermitage
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = camaldolese
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:camaldolese }
			if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
				title:b_lugoItaly = { title:c_ravenna.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:camaldolese } # Sant'Apollinare in Classe
				title:b_isili = { title:c_arborea.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:camaldolese } # Saccargia and Bonarcado
				title:b_fiesole = { title:c_firenze.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:camaldolese } # San Salvatore di Camaldoli, Florence
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
				title:b_lugoItaly = { title:c_ravenna.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:camaldolese } # Sant'Apollinare in Classe
				title:b_isili = { title:c_arborea.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:camaldolese } # Saccargia and Bonarcado

			}

			}
			else_if = {

############################################################################################### Ugo Benedictines


	#The imperial abbey of Farfa and its houses, reformed on the Cluniac model by Abbot Hugh (Ugo) of Farfa about 998
	if = {

		}

			}

			}

############################################################################################### Carthusians


	#Hermit monks of the Grande Chartreuse, founded by St Bruno in 1084; the prior of the Grande Chartreuse was prior general of the order
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 1144.10.1
		}
		title:b_chartreuse = { # Grande Chartreuse
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_clair = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Clair de Fontenay, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_boson = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Boson, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1286.3.15 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_boson = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Boson, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1258.2.15 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_riffier = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Riffier, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_hugues = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Hugues, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1211.6.16 }
			character:MB_chartreuse_jancelin = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Jancelin, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}

			limit = { game_start_date >= 1204.5.16 }

		}

			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }

		}
		else = {
			character:MB_chartreuse_anthelme = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Anthelme de Chignin, prior of the Grande Chartreuse
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1178.10.1
					game_start_date < 1187.7.3
				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_witham_hugues # Hugues d'Avalon, procurator of the Grande Chartreuse
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1246.6.13

				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_portes_bernard # Bernard de La Tour, former prior of Portes
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = carthusian
				}
			}
		}

############################################################################################### Studites


	#Studites
	#The monastery of Stoudios in Constantinople, reformed by St Theodore the Studite from 798; his rule spread across the Byzantine world and to the Rus. Sacked in 1204 and deserted under the Latins, it was restored by Constantine Palaiologos c. 1290-1293
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					game_start_date >= 867.1.1
					game_start_date < 1204.5.16
				}
				game_start_date >= 1300.9.10
			}
		}
		title:b_herakleiaperinthos = { # Stoudios
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = studite_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
			character:MB_stoudios_neophytos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Neophytos, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
			character:MB_stoudios_theoktistos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Theoktistos, hegumen of the restored Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
			character:MB_stoudios_gerasimos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Gerasimos, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }
			character:MB_stoudios_gerasimos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Gerasimos, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1178.10.1 }
			character:MB_stoudios_ioannikios = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Ioannikios, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1144.10.1 }
			character:MB_stoudios_theophylaktos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Theophylaktos, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1081.4.1 }
			character:studite_2 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Niketas Stethatos, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1066.9.15 }
			character:MB_stoudios_michael = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Michael, synkellos and hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1018.8.1 }
			character:melkite.031 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Nicholas, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 936.8.7 }
			character:MB_stoudios_anatolios = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Anatolios, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		else = {
			character:MB_stoudios_sabas = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Sabas, hegumen of Stoudios
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add now-unneeded abbots as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1066.9.15
					game_start_date < 1081.4.1
				}
				add_courtier = character:studite_2 # Niketas Stethatos, monk of Stoudios and biographer of Symeon the New Theologian
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1018.8.1
					game_start_date < 1066.9.15
				}
				add_courtier = character:70542 # Alexios, monk of Stoudios and later hegumen and Patriarch
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 867.1.1
					game_start_date < 936.8.7
				}
				add_courtier = character:studite_1 # Nicholas the Confessor, the deposed hegumen, held at Stoudios
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = studite
				}
			}
		}
	}

			character:MB_athos_stephanos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Stephanos, protos of the Holy Mountain
		}
		else = {
			character:athonite_1 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Euthymios the Younger, hermit of the Holy Mountain
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add notable monks of the Holy Mountain as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1337.5.24
				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_gregory_palamas # Gregory Palamas, hesychast monk of the Lavra hermitage of St Sabbas
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1286.3.15
					game_start_date < 1300.9.10
				}
				add_courtier = character:MB_athos_nikephoros_hesychast # Nikephoros the Hesychast, teacher of the prayer of the heart
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1194.9.30
					game_start_date < 1211.6.16
				}
				add_courtier = character:218508 # Sava, son of Stefan Nemanja, monk of Vatopedi and founder of Hilandar
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = athonite
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:athonite }
			if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
				title:b_gynaikokastron = { title:c_thessalonika.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:athonite } # St Andrew at Peristerai, given to the Lavra in 964
				title:b_kassandreia = { title:c_chalkidike.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:athonite } # Hagios Mamas, granted to Vatopedi in 1301
			}
			else_if = {

			}
			else_if = {

############################################################################################### Bithynians


	#Bithynians
	#The monastic communities of the Bithynian Olympus and the other holy mountains of Bithynia; Olympus never recovered from the Turkish raids after 1071, and by the 1190s its remaining monks were under the hegumen of Elegmoi as archimandrite of Olympus
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				game_start_date < 1081.4.1

				}
			}

					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = bithynian_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}

			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
			character:bithynian_2 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Isaakios, hegumen of Elegmoi and archimandrite of Olympus
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }

		}
		else_if = {

############################################################################################### Evergetine Cenobites


	#Evergetine Cenobites
	#The reforming monastery of the Theotokos Evergetis outside Constantinople, whose typikon was taken up by Kecharitomene, Kosmosoteira, Machairas, Hilandar and many others; after the Latin conquest the movement is carried on by Kosmosoteira at Pherrai
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					game_start_date >= 1066.9.15
					game_start_date < 1204.5.16
				}
				game_start_date >= 1246.6.13
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
			title:b_kypsela = { # Kosmosoteira
				save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			}
		}
		else = {
			title:b_chariopolis = { # Evergetis
				save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_barony = {
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder
					days = 10
				}
				set_variable = {
					name = evergetine_founder
					days = 10
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1337.5.24 }
			character:MB_kosmosoteira_ioannes = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Ioannes, hegumen of Kosmosoteira
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1300.9.10 }
			character:MB_kosmosoteira_kallistos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Kallistos, hegumen of Kosmosoteira
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1286.3.15 }
			character:MB_kosmosoteira_kallistos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Kallistos, hegumen of Kosmosoteira
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1258.2.15 }
			character:MB_kosmosoteira_ioakeim = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Ioakeim, hegumen of Kosmosoteira
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
			character:MB_kosmosoteira_ioakeim = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Ioakeim, hegumen of Kosmosoteira
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
			character:MB_evergetis_hilarion = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Hilarion, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }
			character:MB_evergetis_hilarion = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Hilarion, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1178.10.1 }
			character:MB_evergetis_hilarion = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Hilarion, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1144.10.1 }
			character:MB_evergetis_theodoulos = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Theodoulos, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { game_start_date >= 1081.4.1 }
			character:MB_evergetis_athanasios = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Athanasios, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		else = {
			character:evergetine_1 = { save_scope_as = monastic_leader } # Timothy, hegumen of Evergetis
		}
		scope:game_start_founder = {
			create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
				LEADER = scope:monastic_leader
				CAPITAL = scope:monastic_barony
				NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
				FOUNDER = scope:game_start_founder
				ORDER_TYPE = monastic
			}
		}
		scope:monastic_leader = {
			#Add notable monastics of the Evergetine houses as courtiers
			if = {
				limit = {
					game_start_date >= 1178.10.1
					game_start_date < 1187.7.3
				}
				add_courtier = character:evergetine_2 # Xene, hegumene of the Kecharitomene nunnery
			}
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = devoted
				add_trait_xp = {
					trait = devoted
					value = { 20 80 }
				}
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:monastic_leader
					}
					save_scope_as = evergetine
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = { exists = scope:evergetine }
			if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1246.6.13 }
				# Areia and Machairas lie under Latin lords, and Kosmosoteira is now the seat of the order
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1194.9.30 }
				title:b_kypsela = { title:c_ainos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Kosmosoteira at Pherrai, founded by the sebastokrator Isaac Komnenos in 1152
				title:b_damala = { title:c_korinthos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Areia near Nauplia, founded by Leo of Argos
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1187.7.3 }
				title:b_kypsela = { title:c_ainos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Kosmosoteira at Pherrai, founded by the sebastokrator Isaac Komnenos in 1152
				title:b_stavrovouni = { title:c_nicosia.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Machairas, endowed by Manuel I in 1172
				title:b_damala = { title:c_korinthos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Areia near Nauplia, founded by Leo of Argos
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { game_start_date >= 1178.10.1 }
				title:b_kypsela = { title:c_ainos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Kosmosoteira at Pherrai, founded by the sebastokrator Isaac Komnenos in 1152
				title:b_stavrovouni = { title:c_nicosia.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Machairas, endowed by Manuel I in 1172
				title:b_damala = { title:c_korinthos.holder = { get_title = prev } lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:evergetine } # Areia near Nauplia, founded by Leo of Argos
			}
		}
	}
	
	
############################################################################################### Klimentine Monasteries


	#Klimentine Monasteries
	#The Slavonic monasteries of Bulgaria and Macedonia in the tradition of St Clement of Ohrid, who taught in Kutmichevitsa around Devol from 886: Rila, Pčinja, Osogovo, Ivanovo and the rest
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 936.8.7
		}
		title:b_devol = { # Devol
			save_scope_as = monastic_barony
			county.holder = {
				save_scope_as = game_start_founder
				set_variable = {
					name = monastic_game_start_founder

			}

set_up_historical_military_orders_effect = {
	#CHRISTIAN MILITARY ORDERS


############################################################################################### Knights Templar


	#Knights Templar - 1144.10.1
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 1144.1.1
			game_start_date <= 1144.12.31
		}
		character:Craon3 = { # Robert de Craon
			save_scope_as = leader
		}
		character:231067 = { # Baudouin III, King of Jerusalem
			save_scope_as = founder
			set_variable = {
				name = military_order_game_start_founder
				days = 10
			}
		}
		title:b_towerdavid = { # Templum Salomonis, Jerusalem
			save_scope_as = barony
		}
		create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
			LEADER = scope:leader
			CAPITAL = scope:barony
			NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
			FOUNDER = scope:founder
			ORDER_TYPE = military
		}
		scope:leader = {
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = order_member
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:leader
					}
					save_scope_as = templar
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:templar
			}
			title:b_obidos = { # Soure and Pombal
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = title:c_leiria.holder
					change = scope:change
				}
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:templar
			}
		}
		# Grandmasters/members
		character:144608 = { set_employer = scope:leader } # Everard des Barres, Master in France
		character:144610 = { set_employer = scope:leader } # André de Montbard
		character:144609 = { set_employer = scope:leader } # Bernard de Tremelay
	}
	#Knights Templar - 1178.10.1
	if = {
		limit = {
			game_start_date >= 1178.1.1
			game_start_date <= 1178.12.31
		}
		character:144612 = { # Odo de Saint-Amand
			save_scope_as = leader
		}
		character:223523 = { # Baudouin IV, King of Jerusalem
			save_scope_as = founder
			set_variable = {
				name = military_order_game_start_founder
				days = 10
			}
		}
		title:b_towerdavid = { # Templum Salomonis, Jerusalem
			save_scope_as = barony
		}
		create_holy_order_neutral_effect = {
			LEADER = scope:leader
			CAPITAL = scope:barony
			NEW_HO_SCOPE = new_holy_order
			FOUNDER = scope:founder
			ORDER_TYPE = military
		}
		scope:leader = {
			every_courtier = {
				add_trait = order_member
			}
			faith = {
				random_faith_holy_order = {
					limit = {
						leader = scope:leader
					}
					save_scope_as = templar
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:templar
			}
			title:b_ghazza = { # Gaza
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = title:c_ghazza.holder
					change = scope:change
				}
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:templar
			}
			title:b_baghras = { # Gaston
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = title:c_alexandretta.holder
					change = scope:change
				}
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:templar
			}
			title:b_maials = { # Gardeny
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = title:c_lleida.holder
					change = scope:change

						leader = scope:leader_19
					}
					save_scope_as = al_andalus
				}
			}
		}
		#Lease out additional holdings
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = scope:al_andalus
			}
			title:b_andujar = { # Andujar
				create_title_and_vassal_change = {
					type = revoked
					save_scope_as = change
					add_claim_on_loss = no
				}
				change_title_holder = {
					holder = title:c_andujar.holder
					change = scope:change
				}
				resolve_title_and_vassal_change = scope:change
				lease_out_to_holy_order = scope:al_andalus
			}
		}
	}
